【中信證券：上半年算力鏈業績高增，軟硬體表現分化】中信證券研報稱，2026年上半年科技板塊業績高增、利潤增速顯著跑贏收入，核心驅動力是AI算力需求爆發帶來的全產業鏈量價齊升，業績兌現度最高的環節集中在半導體、通訊裝置、計算機裝置。7月回撥後科技板塊擁擠度與估值壓力邊際緩解，半年報業績高增持續消化估值，行情由估值驅動轉向盈利驅動。分行業來看業績出現分化，電子、計算機行業營收與利潤增速領跑，通訊、傳媒行業整體保持穩健，汽車行業利潤負增長相對承壓。AI算力鏈（儲存、PCB/CCL、光通訊、AI伺服器等）貢獻大部分增量，國產算力、半導體裝置、AI應用、AI終端等環節增長可期。

### 2026-09-11 08:27:56  #MKT News #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bearish #DXY

【Carry-trade unwind accelerates; Edmond de Rothschild bullish on yen】Edmond de Rothschild Asset Management says recent yen gains signal the start of a broader correction to a long-held undervaluation. Strategy chief Michael Nizard wrote that for years the yen was driven mainly by the US-Japan rate gap and its role as a funding currency for global carry trades; it is now trading increasingly on its own fundamentals. He added the yen may be resuming its role as a fundamentals‑driven currency and that any pullbacks in yen crosses should increasingly be treated as opportunities to rebuild or add long yen positions.

### 2026-09-11 08:27:01  #公司 #觀點

【中信證券：需求高景氣、盈利再修復，鋰電產業鏈配置價值凸顯】中信證券研報稱，從電池與能源管理產業鏈2026年上半年及2026年第二季度經營結果來看，下游需求保持高景氣，產業鏈多數環節收入實現增長，盈利修復進一步向中上游擴散。盈利端，電池龍頭盈利韌性較強，碳酸鋰、正極、電解液及六氟、銅箔&鋁箔等環節盈利明顯修復。供給端，行業“固定資產+在建工程”同比增速自2025年第一季度見底後持續回升，2026年第二季度升至16.9%，同時資本開支呈現明顯分化，電池、正極、負極、結構件等環節同比增長，而部分環節仍同比下降，產業鏈逐步由全面收縮轉向結構性投入。考慮到中國鋰電產業鏈全球競爭優勢顯著、海外佈局逐步完善，同時固態、快充等新技術持續迭代，中信證券認為頭部企業有望憑藉技術、成本及全球化優勢進一步擴大份額和盈利優勢，持續看好產業鏈各環節優質頭部公司。

【金價邁向周度下跌 因油價和通脹推高加息預期】著油價飆升和最新的美國PPI資料強化了美聯儲下週加息的可能性，金價邁向連續第三週下跌。金價週五基本持平於每盎司4315美元附近，隔夜下跌1.8%，至8月初以來的最低水平。週四公佈的資料顯示，美國PPI上月上升0.4%，創5月以來最大升幅。這反映出隨著中東衝突升級，來自能源價格上漲的壓力日益增大。現貨黃金下跌0.1%至每盎司4312.20美元，本週累計跌幅或達2.7%。銀價下跌0.3%，至每盎司63.40美元；週四跌5.5%，創下6月以來最大單日跌幅。鉑金和鈀金價格也走低。

### 2026-09-11 08:16:06  #國際

【OpenAI終止“1美元年費”協議 向美國政府機構提供模型五折優惠】據美國總務管理局(GSA)宣告，OpenAI將結束一項允許政府機構每年僅支付1美元即可使用其模型的試點計劃，轉而採用按使用量計費模式，聯邦僱員將能夠以標準價格五折的優惠使用相關技術。為了推動其模型普及，OpenAI於去年8月加入GSA採購平臺OneGov。該平臺允許聯邦機構大規模採購人工智慧軟體。媒體此前報道稱，首年每年1美元的協議，是美國政府中央採購機構迄今談成的最大幅度科技產品折扣。GSA表示，在此期間，350萬名聯邦僱員獲得了ChatGPT使用許可權，僅ChatGPT和其他AI模型就幫助政府節省了14億美元成本。儘管新方案較去年的協議有所漲價，但五折優惠旨在幫助各機構更容易測試和推廣人工智慧工具，包括OpenAI最新的GPT-6 Astra模型。OpenAI計劃於週四宣佈，將把這一優惠從聯邦政府擴大至州政府、地方政府及部落政府。這項與美國政府達成的協議將於10月1日生效，有效期超過兩年。

### 2026-09-11 08:15:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Holds Drop Ahead of US CPI Report — Silver traded around $63.5 an ounce on Friday after tumbling over 5% in the previous session, as investors braced for the US consumer price index report that could reinforce expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike next week. Data released Thursday showed US producer prices accelerated in August as the Iran war pushed wholesale energy costs higher. Markets are now pricing in roughly a 71% probability of a 25-basis-point Fed rate increase next week, up from 61% before the PPI data. Silver also faced pressure from surging oil prices amid the escalating US-Iran conflict, fueling concerns over rising inflation. Meanwhile, Treasury yields jumped following lower-than-expected purchases by the US Treasury Department during its first expanded buyback operation. Non-interest-bearing assets such as silver tend to underperform when yields rise. Silver is on track to lose about 4% this week, marking its third straight weekly decline.

### 2026-09-11 08:07:33  #公司 #市場 #國際

【全球國債收益率飆升 對沖基金Andromeda示警信貸緊縮並稱或已撞上冰山】總部位於倫敦的信貸對沖基金Andromeda Capital Management表示，全球政府債券收益率上升正對公司債市場構成日益嚴重的威脅。該公司投資長兼聯合創始人Alberto Gallo表示，美國政府試圖在不去平衡預算或減少債務的情況下，維持美元強勢並壓低美國國債收益率。他說，但公共借貸規模巨大，赤字不斷膨脹，這種做法難以為繼。“要麼得讓長期收益率上升；這可能導致信貸緊縮，尤其是在AI領域借貸如此頻繁的情況下，要麼得讓貨幣貶值，”Gallo表示。 “政府債券收益率如此之高，我們可能已經撞上了一些冰山，”Gallo說道，他將這種情況比作泰坦尼克號的災難。

### 2026-09-11 08:00:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Producer Prices Change #Importance 2

Japan Producer Prices Rise More than Estimated — Japan’s producer prices rose 7.6% year-on-year in August 2026, following an upwardly revised 7.7% increase in the prior month, which had been the fastest pace since February 2023. The latest reading was above market expectations of 7.4%, indicating commodity and energy cost pressures. Prices continued to grow for all components, including transport equipment (2.4% vs 2.6% in July), drinks and foods (4.3% vs 4.0%), chemicals (13.9% vs 13.8%), petroleum & coal (14.7% vs 17.6%), iron & steel (2.4% vs 2.1%), electrical machinery (4.1% vs 4.9%), production machinery (3.9% vs 3.4%), metal products (3.5% vs 2.0%), electronic components (3.3% vs 3.4%), and information and communications (19.5% vs 18.8%), and textile (2.2% vs 1.8%). On a monthly basis, producer prices fell 0.2%, reversing an upwardly revised 0.4% gain in July and marking the first monthly decline in a year.

### 2026-09-11 07:57:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Business Survey Index Large Manufacturing Firms #Importance 2

Japan Business Sentiment Highest Since 2021 — Japan’s Business Survey Index for large manufacturers rose to 7.6% in the third quarter of 2026 from -1.8% in Q2, far above market expectations of 2.5%. It was the fastest pace of improvement since Q4 2021, amid government efforts to boost GDP growth and stronger government spending. Looking ahead, manufacturing sentiment is expected to continue to expand, though at a softer rate, to 5.8% in Q4 and 4.9% in Q1 2027. The index, based on a survey of major manufacturers, remains a key barometer for an economy where industrial output plays a central role. The Bank of Japan raised its FY2026 GDP growth projection to 0.6% from 0.5% at its latest meeting, due to resilient domestic demand amid the government’s support measures. However, the board warned that underlying inflation could exceed the 2% target amid the ongoing conflict in the Middle East. For FY2027, the BoJ lifted its inflation forecast to 2.4% from 2.3%, while raising its GDP growth outlook to 0.8% from 0.7%.

【中信建投：“六張網”有望兼具穩增長、產業升級與長期資產供給三重作用】中信建投釋出研報稱，“十五五”時期，“六張網”有望兼具穩增長、產業升級與長期資產供給三重作用。基準情形下“十五五”期間約27.93萬億元的“六張網”總投資預計可帶動約149.93萬億元產業鏈總產值、拉動約53.44萬億元GDP，約佔同期GDP總量的6.2%—6.8%，各網在總量貢獻、產業鏈擴散和GDP拉動能力上存在差異。當前政策部署已明顯強化，2026年以來六網投資端率先修復，但結構分化明顯，投資景氣和實物工作量尚未形成持續共振，後續關鍵在於資金、專案、訂單向實體建設的連續傳導。產業端看，傳統基礎設施以工程建設和存量更新為主，新型基礎設施更突出裝置擴容與技術升級；市場端看，政策定價明顯分化，算力產業鏈彈性最強，後續定價將由政策預期轉向訂單和業績驗證。

US Futures Stabilize Ahead of CPI Report — US stock futures steadied on Friday after the major averages declined in the previous session, as investors awaited the August consumer price index report. A hotter-than-expected consumer inflation reading could cement expectations that the Federal Reserve will raise interest rates next week, following Thursday’s data showing producer inflation accelerated last month as the Iran war drove up wholesale energy prices. In regular trading on Thursday, the Dow fell 0.61%, while the S&P 500 and Nasdaq Composite declined 0.59% and 0.65%, respectively. All three benchmarks extended their losing streaks to four sessions. Stocks came under pressure from surging oil prices and Treasury yields as inflation concerns intensified. In corporate news, Oracle gained more than 4% in extended trading after reporting better-than-expected quarterly results, while Adobe fell over 2% on weak guidance for the current quarter.

【Bessent rejects claims he's at odds with Fed chair Warsh, calls it "nonsense"】U.S. Treasury Secretary Bessent rejected claims his actions in the Treasury market conflict with Fed Chair Kevin Warsh, calling the characterization "nonsense." Warsh said in July markets should price without Fed policy guidance, adding market participants are learning to focus on the game itself rather than the referee. Bessent said investors have not demanded an extra risk premium for long-term Treasuries and dismissed related concerns as "unfounded noise." Since the U.S. began military action against Iran, investors have pushed Treasury yields higher: the 10-year yield was below 4% on Feb. 28, the day before the U.S.'s first strike, and rose to 4.96% by Thursday evening, while the 30-year yield sits near its highest level since 2007.

【多因素共振推升水泥價格】步入9月份，國內水泥行業迎來傳統旺季，全國多地開啟水泥價格集中上調模式。目前，全國七大區域17個省份同步落地調價政策，形成年內罕見的全面普漲格局，本輪行情的覆蓋範圍、企業參與度與區域價差幅度，均創下年內階段性新高。據百年建築網監測資料，本輪漲價覆蓋全國核心產銷區域，區域漲幅分化特徵顯著。其中，西南地區漲幅領跑全國，雲南水泥單價上調100元/噸，為全國最大漲幅；北方地區穩步跟漲，河北石家莊、內蒙古東部漲價50元/噸，山西、陝西、甘肅等地漲幅維持在30元/噸至50元/噸；華東地區漲幅相對溫和，江蘇、安徽、江西等省份漲幅僅10元/噸至20元/噸。 “煤炭成本上漲、錯峰停窯供給收縮、‘金九銀十’旺季需求改善，三重因素疊加推動全國水泥價格集中上漲。其中，煤炭成本上漲是主要推手。”百年建築網高階分析師江元林在接受記者採訪時表示。（證券日報）

### 2026-09-11 07:08:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Peru #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Peru Keeps Rates Steady for 12th Straight Meeting — Peru’s Central Reserve Bank kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 4.25% in September 2026, extending its pause for a 12th consecutive meeting. Annual inflation rose to 4.4% in August from 4.1% in July, mainly due to a base effect from negative monthly inflation a year earlier, while inflation excluding food and energy eased to 4.5% from 4.6%. Excluding transportation, underlying inflation stood at 1.8% and has remained below 2% since April 2025. Twelve-month inflation expectations increased to 3.1% from 3.0%, slightly above the upper limit of the target range. The central bank expects inflation to return toward 2% as supply shocks fade, although El Niño and Middle East tensions could keep inflation pressures elevated. Economic activity remained strong in August, with all current-conditions indicators improving and expectations staying optimistic, though somewhat more moderate. Global risks remain elevated, reflected in greater financial-market and oil-price volatility.

【Nissan reorganizes Japan production, maintains 1.0 mln domestic capacity】Nikkei reports Nissan will close one plant and reallocate output across remaining factories to keep Japanese production capacity at about 1.0 mln vehicles. Van production from two Fukuoka plants will be shifted to the Tochigi plant over the next 2–3 years. Nissan’s Oppama plant in Kanagawa is scheduled to cease operations at the end of fiscal 2027. The move is part of a global plan to close and restructure seven assembly plants. After Oppama shuts, Nissan’s direct Japan capacity will drop to roughly 800k; including vehicles produced for Nissan by Mitsubishi under contract, domestic capacity will remain near 1.0 mln.

【BESSENT downplays concern after Treasury buyback undershoots】BESSENT downplayed market concern after Thursday’s Treasury buyback fell short of the announced maximum and after a recent rise in U.S. Treasury yields, saying the market is “in very good shape.” Two-year yields hit their highest levels since 2024 and 10-year yields rose to highs not seen since 2023. He said the sell-off was driven by a surge in oil prices and by the Treasury repurchasing fewer securities than planned: the Treasury had announced buybacks of up to $6.0 bln but bought $5.19 bln on Thursday. “We only buy when prices are cheap,” he said, adding that holders preferred to keep long-dated paper; the Treasury typically receives about $20.0 bln of offers to sell but this time received roughly $10.0 bln.

President Trump has urged ExxonMobil, ConocoPhillips and Harold Hamm's Continental Resources to commit about $100 billion to restore Venezuelan fields. Eight months after the US captured Venezuela's president Maduro, the major oil companies have largely remained on the sidelines; Primavera is among the few US firms actively participating.

【AI需求推動甲骨文雲業務營收超預期增長 股價盤後大漲】甲骨文雲端計算業務增長超過華爾街預期，顯示該公司對大型人工智慧(AI)資料中心專案的鉅額投入正在取得成效。其股價盤後大漲逾6%。甲骨文週四盤後公佈財報，備受關注的雲基礎設施業務上財季營收同比大增121%至74億美元，高於分析師預測均值71.9億美元。甲骨文正從傳統資料庫軟體供應商轉型為AI算力提供商，併為OpenAI等客戶大舉建設資料中心。由於投資者此前擔憂融資需求、零部件成本上升以及資料中心建設面臨挑戰，該公司股價自6月1日創下的年內高點大跌38%。截至8月31日的財季，甲骨文資本支出達到285億美元，主要用於資料中心裝置。首席財務官Hilary Maxson重申，截至2027年5月的財年資本支出預計為700億美元，此外還將有200億至250億美元用於部分零部件的預付款。甲骨文表示，上財季新增AI雲合同超過300億美元，推動代表未來收入的剩餘履約義務進一步增加。

【Microsoft to more than triple global data‑center capacity to over 38 GW by 2032 to ease AI compute shortage】People familiar with the matter said Microsoft (MSFT.O) plans to expand global data‑center capacity to more than 38 GW by 2032 from about 12 GW today — a greater‑than threefold increase — to address compute shortages driven by rapid AI and cloud demand. The roadmap covers owned and leased data centers but excludes compute rented from newer cloud providers such as CoreWeave. The plan remains subject to customer demand and technological change. Sources said Microsoft previously limited some data‑center builds, turned away certain AI and cloud workloads, and restricted new cloud subscriptions in parts of the US and Europe, prompting some customers to migrate to competitors. Microsoft said it is accelerating data‑center construction. The company’s latest fiscal‑year capital expenditure was $145.0 bln; analysts expect capex to rise in coming years.

### 2026-09-11 05:12:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #El Salvador #Producer Prices Change #Importance 0

El Salvador PPI Slows in August — Producer prices in El Salvador rose 3.52% year-on-year in August 2026, easing slightly from a 3.54% increase in July. The slowdown was largely driven by lower price growth in manufacturing (3% vs. 3.14% in July) and transport and warehousing (4.59% vs. 8.35%). Meanwhile, prices rose at a faster pace for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (23.38% vs. 23.05%), lodging and food services (2.62% vs. 2.02%), real estate (4.18% vs. 1.77%) and professional, scientific and technical activities (1.56% vs. 1.43%). Information and telecommunications prices were unchanged, while education slowed to 1.80% from 2.82%. Producer prices continued to decline for water supply, sewerage and waste management (-0.15%), while healthcare prices fell 2.39% from 2.49% in July.

### 2026-09-11 05:08:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Crude Rises as US-Iran Conflict Widens — Brent crude past $108 a barrel on Thursday, its highest level since May 19th, as escalating tensions in the Middle East raised fears of prolonged disruptions to global energy supplies. Top White House advisers reportedly discussed with President Donald Trump the possibility that the Iran war could drag on past Inauguration Day in January 2029. Iran’s Houthi allies in Yemen struck several energy facilities in Saudi Arabia this week, raising concerns that the conflict was broadening. Saudi Arabia’s crude oil production fell sharply in August 2026, dropping by around 1.9 million barrels a day. Iran reported strikes on US warships and tankers in the Persian Gulf, while the US said it had hit 10 Iranian tankers. Adding to upward pressure on prices, oil tanker rates have jumped to record highs, while US crude inventories fell by 300,000 barrels in the week ended September 4th. In contrast, the EIA raised its forecast for US crude oil production in 2027 to 14.3 million barrels per day.

【Houthi forces seize Yemen's Hanish Islands, capture Mocha port】A Yemeni government official said on the 10th that Houthi forces deployed to the strategic Hanish Islands in the Red Sea after government naval units withdrew; the government later designated part of the west coast a "combat zone." The official said the navy pulled back in the afternoon, after which Houthi fighters entered and established military positions on the islands. The Hanish Islands lie north of the Bab al-Mandeb Strait, close to major international shipping routes and are strategically important for monitoring and securing maritime traffic. Also on the 10th, after hours of heavy fighting, Houthi forces seized the Red Sea port city of Mocha in southwestern Taiz province, forcing government troops to withdraw south to the Zubab area between Mocha and the Bab al-Mandeb Strait.

Brazilian Stocks Rise on Election Outlook — The Ibovespa rose 1.4% to close at 188,269 on Thursday, erasing earlier losses as the market priced in a higher probability of a change in power in October’s presidential election. Recent polls have shown Flávio Bolsonaro gaining ground in the presidential race, while the shift in odds on Polymarket added further momentum to the move on Thursday. Markets associate a potential Flávio Bolsonaro victory with expectations of greater fiscal austerity, while elevated domestic yields and weak business activity continue to weigh on the economic outlook. Gains were broad-based, with major banks posting sharp advances: Itaú rose 1.8%, Bradesco gained 3.9%, and Banco do Brasil added 2.8%. Utilities also closed higher, with Sabesp up 2.2%. Rede D’Or (+3.1%) also outperformed. Petrobras rose 1.4% on higher oil prices, while Vale shed 1.1% as iron ore prices fell.

【ANTHROPIC discloses dangerous Claude use cases; says Iran-linked account drafted US naval attack targets】ANTHROPIC said it blocked attempts by some researchers to use its Claude AI model for tasks that could "support biological weapons development", according to a detailed report. The report lists five cases, including one on enhancing transmission of a mosquito-borne disease. The company said it could not conclusively determine whether the cases were linked to a weapons program but blocked the activity and notified authorities and industry partners. ANTHROPIC also reported Claude being used for surveillance, fraud, conventional-weapons research and propaganda, and said it had identified and banned influence-operation accounts, including three linked to Iran; one Iran-linked actor used Claude to draft suggested attack targets against US naval forces in the region.

### 2026-09-11 04:14:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

Argentina Monthly Inflation Eases in August — Argentina’s consumer price index rose 1.7% month-over-month in August 2026, easing from 2.1% in July, matching forecasts. Housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels recorded the largest monthly increase at 2.8%, followed by education at 2.5%. Recreation and culture was unchanged, while clothing and footwear fell 0.6%. Food and non-alcoholic beverages made the largest contribution to monthly inflation across regions, driven by higher prices for vegetables, tubers, legumes, fruit, bread and cereals. Regulated prices rose 2.2%, led by increases in electricity, gas, public transportation and private healthcare costs. Seasonal prices fell 0.9%, as declines in tour packages and clothing were partly offset by higher prices for vegetables, tubers, legumes and fruit. Year-to-date inflation reached 21.3%. Core CPI increased 1.8%.

### 2026-09-11 03:54:48  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #US10Y #Impact bearish #DXY #Impact bullish #S&P500

【Treasury buys below cap in Bessent’s expanded buyback; 10-year yield highest since 2023】U.S. Treasury bought $5.187 bln of 10- to 20-year notes in Thursday’s buyback, below the announced $6.0 bln cap and below market expectations, exacerbating the sell-off and pushing the 10-year yield to its highest level since 2023. Investors submitted about $10.5 bln of offers. While the Treasury is not required to fill the cap, this is the third time in 53 long-term buybacks since the program’s 2024 relaunch that it did not purchase the full amount. TD Securities strategist Molly Brooks said the Treasury is being more selective than usual; to meet market expectations and absorb the full allotment it may need to accept less attractive prices in future operations, and the precedent of 100% take-up has been broken, which could help reset market expectations.

【US media: US military boosts intelligence support to Saudi Arabia while avoiding direct combat】CNN, citing multiple sources, reports more than 100 U.S. military advisers are deployed in Saudi Arabia to provide intelligence and targeting support against Yemen’s Houthi movement. The personnel are part of a recently formed joint force command totaling about 200. The U.S. has provided target-location tools enabling near‑real‑time battlefield visibility and geospatial strike support, but is not directly conducting airstrikes or supplying aerial refueling or other combat support. The assistance is described as aimed at helping Saudi forces operate more smoothly and professionally. The expansion of support comes as Houthi forces press advances on the Arabian Peninsula, with fighting at its most intense since 2022; the Houthis recently captured a strategic Red Sea port, raising U.S. and Saudi concern about a possible second front. U.S. officials say they assess there are hundreds of Iran’s Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps personnel in Yemen working with the Houthis, reportedly with an objective to close the Bab al‑Mandeb. U.S. military leaders remain cautious about deeper involvement over the risk of civilian casualties and political fallout.

【隨著戰爭創造需求，美國一種關鍵中質含硫原油價格飆升】根據Link Data Resources的資料，美國中質含硫原油品種Mars週三飆升至自4月以來相對於西得克薩斯中質原油（WTI）的最強價差。該品種達到每桶6.25美元的溢價。過去兩個交易日每桶均上漲超過2美元。Mars原油與伊朗戰爭帶來的波動性密切相關，因為全球買家正在尋找替代品種，以彌補因霍爾木茲海峽受阻而損失的原油供應。4月份戰爭初期，該品種曾飆升至18美元的溢價。

【Strong demand at U.S. $22 bln 30-year Treasury auction eases long end; 30-year yield retreats from session highs】Strong demand at the U.S. $22 bln 30-year Treasury auction eased long-end pressure for a second straight day as the stop-out yield came in below traders’ expectations. BMO said primary dealer take-up fell to a record low, implying greater absorption by end investors. The 30-year yield fell most after the sale, retreating to about 5.33% from intraday highs but remained notably higher on the day; the 10-year was roughly unchanged at 4.924%. Short-dated Treasuries saw no lift from the auction and yields ticked up slightly, indicating the bid primarily relieved long-end selling pressure. Longer maturities remain pressured by U.S. fiscal deficits, elevated issuance and inflation concerns.

### 2026-09-11 01:10:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 0

Swiss Franc Steady Near Three-Week Low — The Swiss franc weakened past 0.81 per USD, remaining near a three-week low, on a widening interest rate differential with the United States and selling pressure from new carry trades. A hawkish sentiment of the Bank of Japan and yen-buying interventions by Washington and Tokyo reduced the appeal of the yen for carry trades, leading traders to shift positions to other safe-haven currencies, such as the swiss franc. The swing pressures the currency as traders sell their franc loans for higher-yielding assets in other currencies. Contrasting with other central banks, the Swiss National Bank is expected to leave its key policy rate unchanged at 0% through year-end, the lowest among major economies. Providing an offsetting pressure, global oil prices breached the $100 mark as tensions in the Middle East escalate further, raising safe-haven demand.

【美銀策略師警告AI驅動的股票風險 呼籲投資者“現在”就對沖】美國銀行歐洲股票策略主管Sebastian Raedler表示，投資者現在應該對人工智慧推動的股票敞口進行對沖。他警告稱，過去200年來，每一次重大的投資熱潮最終都以崩盤告終。“這是一場風險極高的遊戲，”這位策略師在接受彭博電視採訪時表示。Raedler稱，許多投資者覺得自己別無選擇，只能參與人工智慧交易，但他們需要做好一旦出現逆轉跡象就迅速行動的準備。“人們很緊張。”Raedler敦促投資者開始佈局那些遭到重挫、但如果這輪熱潮最終被證明不可持續時便會受益的股票。這位策略師還提到，他的衍生品團隊同事提出，可以針對必需消費品等防禦性板塊採取期權策略，作為一種對沖手段。

### 2026-09-11 00:42:12  #MKT News #XAUUSD #Impact bullish #DXY #WTI #Impact mixed

【Reports: Iran resumes ballistic missile production】The Wall Street Journal, citing US and Middle East officials, reports Iran has resumed ballistic missile production, reassembling weapons from pre-existing component stocks in underground facilities. Tehran is producing both liquid- and solid-fuel ballistic missiles at multiple subterranean sites, including the Khojir missile complex, and is attempting to build additional underground assembly points to reduce strike vulnerability. Officials say current output relies on inventory parts and remains below pre-war levels; a US official estimated Iran could assemble at least several hundred missiles from existing components. Analysts note prior stockpiles included warheads, airframes and guidance/control systems, and future output will depend on stock size and the pace of underground repairs—potentially ranging from hundreds to thousands. Pentagon spokesman Parnell said the US has destroyed up to 90% of Iran’s UAV, ballistic missile and naval industrial infrastructure; satellite imagery showing repairs to roads, bridges, production facilities and missile-base tunnel entrances has raised US and Israeli concern that Tehran’s military capabilities are recovering.

### 2026-09-11 00:30:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

CAC 40 Falls Following ECB Rate Hike — The CAC 40 fell 0.5% to close at 8,117 on Thursday following the ECB’s decision to raise its key interest rate to 2.5%. The 25-basis-point hike was anticipated after euro area inflation jumped to 3.3% in August, the highest level in three years and above the ECB’s 2% target. “The conflict in the Middle East continues to generate inflation pressures, and inflation is set to remain well above target for an extended period,” the ECB said in a statement. Reports indicated that ECB officials expect to raise interest rates further, with another increase possible as soon as next month. Meanwhile, oil prices jumped as fears of prolonged energy supply disruptions intensified following the biggest wave of attacks on shipping in the Strait of Hormuz by the US and Iran on Wednesday. Luxury stocks posted losses amid elevated risk aversion, with L’Oréal down 1.6% and LVMH shedding 1.2%. Industrial stocks also closed lower, with Air Liquide and Sanofi down 0.9% and EssilorLuxottica losing 2.1%.

### 2026-09-11 00:28:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Gasoline Climbs to Six-Week High — US gasoline futures rose to around $3.35 a gallon, reaching their highest level since late July, as tensions in the Middle East show no sign of waning, reducing the outlook for refined fuel supply. Tehran stated it would continue counterstrikes if attacks from the US military persist. Adding to the pressure, Saudi Arabia reported that its crude oil output in August plunged to its lowest level since 1990, as the war in the Middle East continued to disrupt its export routes. Saudi and Yemeni officials stated that the Houthis are now nearing complete control of the Bab al-Mandeb Strait, while Russia's energy infrastructure remains pressured from persistent Ukrainian strikes. Meanwhile, EIA data showed that US gasoline inventories rose by 1.269 million barrels in the week ending September 4th, although fuel production declined even as US refineries are operating near full capacity. According to AAA, the average national US regular gasoline price jumped to $4.28 a gallon on September 10th.

### 2026-09-11 00:26:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Stock Market #Importance 1

TSX Falls to Five-Week Low — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell nearly 1% to trade below 36,000 on Thursday, reaching a five-week low as rising oil prices and higher bond yields added to inflationary pressures. Oil prices jumped as fears of prolonged energy supply disruptions intensified following the biggest wave of attacks on shipping in the Strait of Hormuz by Washington and Tehran on Wednesday. Global yields moved higher on fears of prolonged inflation, following hotter-than-expected US PPI data and ahead of Friday’s CPI report. Rising expectations for a Fed rate hike pressured credit-sensitive stocks, with most major banks trading below the flatline. Falling gold prices weighed on miners, with Agnico Eagle losing 2.5%, WPM falling 3%, and Barrick retreating more than 2%. Copper prices also fell, dragging Hudbay down more than 7.5%. Enbridge shed more than 3% after announcing it would buy Tallgrass Energy’s crude oil business, acquiring a majority stake in the Pony Express Pipeline and other assets.

### 2026-09-11 00:14:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Plunges from Record — Copper futures in the US plummeted to $6.45 on Thursday from the record high of $6.5 earlier in the session on reports that the US will backtrack on copper tariffs. The White House is reconsidering its earlier signal to place levies on refined copper products due to high inflation for manufacturers ahead of the midterms. Expectations that the US would add refined copper to tariffs on semi-finished products triggered a surge in global prices and large inflows of metal into North America. This coincided with the ongoing shortages of sulfuric acid for major copper refiners worldwide as supply pressures from GCC countries drove China to halt their exports. Consequently, backwardation curves steepened both in major Western copper exchanges. Mined supply has also faltered with reports from Codelco and Freeport-McMoRan posting double digit declines in production, shortly after the International Copper Study Group pointed to a 1.1% decline in global production in the first half of the year.

### 2026-09-11 00:11:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Denmark Central Bank Follows ECB Rate Hike — Denmark’s central bank increased its key interest rate by 25 basis points to 2.10% in September, following a similar move by the European Central Bank. The ECB raised rates for the second time this year, saying the conflict in the Middle East continues to fuel inflationary pressures. The decision ensures that the interest rate difference between Denmark and the Eurozone remains unchanged, maintaining the stability of Denmark's currency, the krone, against the euro. Denmark's benchmark current account interest rate and the certificate of deposit rate were each hiked by 25 basis points to 2.10% from 2.25%, while the so-called lending rate was cut by 25 basis points to 2.50% from 2.75%.

【法國極左翼提議登出央行所持債務 歐洲央行行長稱金融層面很危險】歐洲央行行長克里斯蒂娜·拉加德表示，極左翼有關透過登出央行所持債務來削減法國鉅額政府債務的方案並不合理，而且在金融層面很危險。法國總統候選人讓-呂克·梅朗雄主張登出法國央行和歐洲央行所持部分法國國債，隨著明年春季選舉臨近，這一提議迄今已成為經濟政策辯論的焦點之一。“我要說的是，一項無論從法律、技術還是金融角度都說不通的提議，並不會因為你不斷重複它就變得合理，”拉加德週四在柏林舉行的新聞釋出會上表示。拉加德舉例稱，這就好比有人拒絕償還貸款，卻還想再次借錢，這個人要麼必須支付高昂的融資成本，要麼根本借不到錢。拉加德表示，Blanchard已經說明，“把錢從一個口袋拿出來放進另一個口袋，並不意味著債務真的被登出了”。她還表示，登出債務的想法“在金融層面是危險的”。’

### 2026-09-10 23:36:27  #市場 #國際

【美債市場出現大額期權交易 押注10年期收益率升破5%】週四美債市場出現了一宗大額期權交易，押注國債拋售勢頭將推動10年期收益率升破5%。油價上漲可能進一步刺激本就高企的通脹，上述期權交易是投資者加緊對沖債市風險的最新跡象。拋售推動10年期收益率再次逼近2023年略高於5%的峰值。30年期美國國債收益率週四升至5.35%，為2007年以來最高水平。這筆期權交易支付的權利金約為1,400萬美元，在衍生品市場上規模相當可觀。此前，交易員稱市場已經出現一波幕後對沖活動，為拋盤推波助瀾。如果跌勢持續，投資者可能進一步買入看空期權，以保護投資組合免受損失，同時也可能增加所謂的凸性對沖。如果10年期美國國債收益率升至約5.1%，這筆交易將達到盈虧平衡點；如果收益率升至5.2%，收益將增至約1,500萬美元。10年期收益率上一次達到這一水平是在2007年。

【White House to send $500 ACA refunds to nearly 1 million ahead of midterms; cost ~$500m】The Trump administration said it will send $500 payments to nearly 1 million Americans it says were overcharged buying coverage on federal Affordable Care Act exchanges. The White House said the checks will be mailed in October, before midterm elections, and attributed the payments to a surplus accumulated during the Biden administration while accusing higher premiums of producing excess user charges. The refunds are estimated by the White House to cost about $500 million. The payments could exacerbate public concerns about inflation and the expanding budget deficit and public debt. Trump has long sought to repeal the ACA and has proposed directly subsidizing individuals to buy insurance rather than paying insurers.

South African Rand at Over 1-Week Low — The South African rand traded around 16.2 per USD, its lowest since late August, pressured by elevated oil prices and a firmer dollar amid rising bets of a Fed rate hike next week. Risk sentiment remained fragile as escalating Middle East hostilities raised fears of a prolonged conflict, sending energy prices higher and clouding the inflation outlook. South Africa's inflation rate eased to 4.3% in July from 5% in June, but higher fuel prices are expected to rekindle price pressures. Meanwhile, recent data underscored the economy’s fragile footing. Mining production plunged a more-than-expected 7.5% in July, while manufacturing output unexpectedly rebounded 1.1% after a 1.8% decline in June. Separately, South Africa's GDP contracted 0.2% in Q2, ending a six-quarter expansion streak. Against this backdrop, the SARB faces a delicate balancing act later this month, weighing the need to keep inflation on track toward its target against the need to support a weakening economy.

【倫銅跳水 報道稱白宮尚未決定是否對精煉銅徵收關稅】10日傍晚，倫銅、紐約期銀價格突然跳水。訊息面上，據媒體報道，兩名知情人士稱，白宮尚未決定是否對精煉銅徵收關稅，官員們正在權衡兩方面的得失：銅價上漲可能會推高製造成本，但鼓勵國內增加採礦也能帶來益處。 訊息人士稱，白宮的銅關稅計劃陷入停滯。隨著美國中期選舉臨近，特朗普和共和黨議員正面臨壓力，他們需要證明自己的經濟政策正在降低美國消費者和企業的成本。美國政府對生活成本問題的日益重視，使得“相關方面出現了猶豫”。 而美國銅關稅正是這一輪銅價上漲的關鍵推手。 由於市場預期特朗普將對陰極銅等精煉銅產品以及銅精礦徵收關稅，交易商和工業買家紛紛在美國囤積庫存，將銅價推至歷史高位。 該報道還援引一名白宮官員的話稱，銅關稅問題尚未做出最終決定，且美國商務部已經在白宮設定的6月30日期限前向特朗普彙報了最新情況。 這名官員同時表示：“政府仍在評估所有選項，以推動銅及其他關鍵製造業回遷美國。”（期貨日報）

【美國二手房銷量降至一年多以來最低 高利率使購房者保持觀望】由於潛在買家等待房貸利率下降，美國8月份二手房銷售放緩至一年多來的最低水平。美國全國房地產經紀人協會(NAR)週四公佈的資料顯示，8月份二手房銷量摺合年率下降2%，為398萬套。自2024年秋季以來，月度摺合年率銷量僅有兩次跌破400萬套，8月份便是其中之一。這組令人失望的資料反映出，房地產市場迫切需要新的推動因素。相比疫情期間高達兩位數的年度漲幅，房價上漲步伐已經放慢，但負擔能力問題仍然是購房者面臨的一個重要阻力。二手房銷售價格中位數較上年同期上漲1.6%，至429,100美元，延續了自2023年年中以來的同比上漲勢頭。NAR首席經濟學家Lawrence Yun在一份宣告中表示：“房貸利率和二手房銷售走勢相反，因此高房貸利率導致購房活動小幅下降並不令人意外。”不過，Yun在與記者的電話會議上表示，面對房貸利率上升，購房“並沒有崩潰”，並指出就業和工資增長幫助支撐了需求。不過，他表示，房貸利率可能很快觸及7%。

Palladium Falls Toward Three-Week Low — Palladium futures fell below $1,315 an ounce, heading toward their lowest level since mid-August, as the metal remained influenced by near-term investment flows. Heightened economic uncertainty stemming from the conflict in the Middle East has raised expectations of monetary tightening across major economies, weighing on demand for the non-yielding metal. The ECB hiked its policy rate at its September meeting, while a rise in US producer inflation led investors to increase bets on a Fed rate hike at its meeting next week. Meanwhile, UBS forecasts a market surplus for palladium, as increased recycling and weaker consumption of catalytic converters are expected to more than offset lower mining output compared with last year. On the other hand, Russian palladium exports to China doubled in the first seven months of the year.

【Tanker shortage, Houthi threat shift ARAMCO product exports to Red Sea refineries】ARMACO has shifted most product exports to Red Sea refineries as tanker scarcity and Houthi attacks slow the recovery of refined flows through the Strait of Hormuz, keeping fuel prices elevated. At least 1.0 mln bpd currently transit Hormuz, Vortexa data show, versus about 4.0 mln bpd pre-war (ex-LPG). Traders at a Singapore meeting said recovery is hampered by difficulty finding tankers to load; owners still carry crude via Hormuz because surging VLCC freight makes voyages profitable, but refined products move on smaller ships that require more trips and carry higher risk. Two industry sources said ARAMCO exports roughly 500k bpd of products daily, mostly from Red Sea coastal refineries; shipments from Ras Tanura remain limited because chartering vessels for that route is difficult.

### 2026-09-10 22:46:22  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Fall to 3-Week Low — US natural gas prices fell below $2.78/MMBtu, reaching a three-week low after the EIA reported a larger-than-expected storage build. Utilities added 40 billion cubic feet to inventories in the week ending September 4, above expectations for a 31-bcf increase and lifting total stocks to 3.254 trillion cubic feet. Inventories are 4.8% above the five-year average, reinforcing concerns over ample supply. Production has also remained strong, with Lower 48 output averaging 112.9 bcfd so far in September, above August’s monthly high. These factors have outweighed support from robust demand. Above-average temperatures across the South and Southeast are expected to sustain air-conditioning demand, while gas deliveries to major LNG export facilities have increased. Rising European and Asian demand for US LNG, as buyers seek alternatives to disrupted Middle Eastern supplies and prepare for winter, is providing additional underlying support to the market.

【華爾街警告美國AI債務擴張對美國債形成壓力】目前，越來越多華爾街機構警告稱，美國AI債務擴張已經開始對美國國債市場形成競爭性壓力。據摩根大通Michael Cembalest估算，2026年迄今五大超大規模雲端計算企業加上英偉達的債務發行規模約為3200億美元（含資料中心租賃特殊目的載體），其中長久期部分摺合十年期等價規模約3030億美元，相當於同期美國財政部新增長久期借款的68%。高盛固定收益信貸交易員、投資級信用專家Jeffrey Papai在最新報告中警告稱，在年內已累計約3000億美元AI相關債券發行之後，2026年第四季度供給將顯著放緩，為AI信用利差提供短暫喘息視窗。然而這一緩和極為短暫，預計2027年超大規模雲端計算企業及晶片廠商的債券發行規模將較2026年再增約40%，達到約3400億美元，屆時市場將面臨更大沖擊。其核心假設包括：2027年超大規模雲端計算企業資本開支約9300億美元；債務融資佔資本開支比例從2026年的約30%升至37.5%；美元融資佔比從約80%降至70%；高階晶片債務從2026年的約350億美元增至500億至750億美元。（券商中國）

【美元錄得兩週來最佳單日表現，生產者物價指數與油價走勢強化美聯儲政策預期】美國生產者物價報告顯示通脹壓力上升、油價走高後，美元大幅走強，兩項因素共同推升市場對美聯儲今年加息的預期。彭博美元即期指數週四一度上漲 0.4%，創下 8 月 28 日以來最大盤中漲幅。美國勞工統計局週四公佈的資料顯示，剔除食品與能源的核心生產者物價指數上月上漲 0.2%，7 月資料修正後為上漲 0.3%。整體 PPI 同比上漲 5.4%，為三個月最大漲幅，受能源價格上漲推動，能源價格暫無回落跡象。受美伊衝突持續升級影響，布倫特原油週四飆升至每桶 105 美元。美國國債收益率上行，全球各國收益率同步走高。蒙特利爾銀行首席外匯策略師馬克・麥考密克表示，油價走高預示 “未來數月通脹壓力將持續存在”。他稱，市場低估了通脹水平及其對利率、美元強勢以及投資者風險偏好的影響。“今夏市場主流觀點是經濟軟著陸，風險偏好保持樂觀。一旦美聯儲相關表態導致市場情緒惡化，美元將進一步走強，且金融環境大機率會在秋季趨於收緊。”

This marked the sixth consecutive month of growth in wholesale inventories and the strongest increase since March. Nondurable goods inventories rose 1.6% in July, rebounding from a 0.3% decline in June, while durable goods inventories increased 1.1%, up from a 0.8% rise in June. On a yearly basis, wholesale inventories advanced by 5.7%, as previously estimated.

### 2026-09-10 22:11:05  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Platinum Falls Toward One-Week Low — Platinum fell below $1,820 an ounce, moving toward its lowest level in a week, as the metal remained influenced by near-term investment flows while traders assessed prospects monetary tightening across major economies, weighing on demand for the non-yielding metal. The World Platinum Investment Council revised its 2026 market forecast to a surplus, with total demand set to fall 18%, driven by reduced demand for Chinese jewelry, which plunged 32%, and from automakers, which declined 6%. Additionally, heightened economic uncertainty stemming from the conflict in the Middle East has increased expectations of monetary tightening across major economies. The ECB hiked its policy rate at its September meeting, while a rise in US producer inflation led investors to increase bets on a Fed rate hike at its meeting next week, further dampening demand for platinum. In the long term, however, industrial demand provides some support, underpinned by the rapid build-out of artificial intelligence.

### 2026-09-10 22:09:36  #央行 #國際

【歐洲央行再次加息】歐洲中央銀行10日結束貨幣政策會議，決定將歐元區三大關鍵利率均上調25個基點。這是歐洲央行今年以來第二次加息。此次加息後，歐元區存款機制利率、主要再融資利率和邊際借貸利率分別為2.50%、2.65%、2.90%。歐洲央行當天釋出新聞公報說，中東衝突持續帶來通脹壓力，預計歐元區通脹率將在較長一段時間內明顯高於該行2%的目標水平。此次加息旨在確保歐元區中期通脹率穩定在目標水平。公報還表示，受能源價格衝擊影響，歐元區經濟前景仍存在高度不確定性，通脹面臨上行風險。歐洲央行最新預測顯示，2026年歐元區整體通脹率預計為3%，與6月預測值持平，2027年通脹預期則上調至2.5%。同時，歐洲央行將歐元區2026年和2027年經濟增長預期分別小幅上調至0.9%和1.4%。歐洲央行行長拉加德表示，中東衝突等地緣政治因素進一步推高能源價格，預計歐元區整體通脹率在2027年上半年仍將明顯高於目標水平。此後，隨著能源價格回落，並在高利率作用下，通脹率有望於明年底前後回到目標水平。今年6月，歐洲央行將歐元區三大關鍵利率均上調25個基點，為2023年9月以來首次加息。（新華社）

### 2026-09-10 22:08:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Existing Home Sales #Importance 2

US Existing Home Sales Fall as Expected — Existing home sales in the United States fell by 2.0% from the previous month to a seasonally adjusted annualized rate of 3.98 million units in August of 2026, aligned with market expectations. It follows a 1.7% decline in the previous months. Existing home sales declined in the Northeast (-4.0%), Midwest (-3.1%), and South (-1.6%) regions while sales were relatively unchanged in the West. Total housing inventory increased 3.2% to 1.62 million units and the median price was $429,100 for all housing types, a 1.6% increase from last year. “Mortgage rates and home sales move in opposite directions, so it's not surprising to see a mild dip in home buying activity due to high mortgage rates,” said NAR Chief Economist Lawrence Yun. Mortgage rates measured by leading mortgage providing institutions continued to rise through the period as soaring energy costs and ample corporate debt supply lifted longer-maturity yields.

【Markets almost evenly split on whether ECB will hike next month】The ECB raised rates 25bp to 2.5% on Thursday and issued a statement seen as hawkish, but markets are almost evenly divided on a further move next month. LSEG pricing shows a 49% probability of a Oct. 29 hike; if officials do not act in October, markets put an 89% chance of a rate increase before Dec. 17 and a 36% probability the deposit rate reaches 3% before Christmas. ECB staff raised 2027–28 inflation forecasts citing the Middle East conflict and upgraded 2026–27 GDP forecasts on unexpectedly strong euro area resilience. ECB President Lagarde warned the outlook remains highly uncertain, saying conditions can change overnight — citing diesel prices — and “we cannot predict the next step.”

Baltic Dry Index Breaks 2-Day Advance — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, snapped a two-day winning streak on Thursday, dropping about 2.7% to a one-week low of 3,521 points. Weaker rates across larger segments were behind the decline. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, also halted a two-day advance, slipping 4.3% to 6,122 points; and the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell 0.2% to 2,409 points. Conversely, the supramax index rose by 0.5% to 1,713 points.

【US yields, dollar strengthen; gold falls over 1%】Gold fell more than 1% on Thursday after a firm US PPI print and rising oil prices boosted bets on a Fed rate hike next week. Capital.com senior analyst Kyle Rodda said August PPI signaled a pickup in underlying inflation partly driven by higher energy costs; headline PPI was broadly in line with forecasts but energy rebounded. CME FedWatch now prices a roughly 70% chance of a hike next week, up from 62% before the data. A Reuters survey of economists still shows most expect the Fed to hold rates at the Sept. 15-16 meeting and for the rest of the year. A stronger dollar and a rise in the 10-year US Treasury yield further pressured dollar-priced gold. Rodda added that higher bond yields reflect more persistent inflation pressure from oil, increasing the opportunity cost of holding non-yielding assets and weighing on gold.

### 2026-09-10 21:44:19  #公司

【SpaceX調整資料中心建設策略 由追求速度轉向強化可靠性】據報道，SpaceX正在調整其AI資料中心建設方式，新管理團隊計劃在設施投入運營前安裝更多電力和冷卻備份系統，並進行更充分測試，同時減少對臨時供電和冷卻裝置的依賴，這可能放慢新資料中心園區的擴張速度。報道稱，這一調整源於xAI此前快速建設模式暴露出的可靠性問題，其田納西州和密西西比州資料中心早期曾頻繁發生故障，導致AI研究專案暫停並損失部分訓練進度。SpaceX今年收購xAI後，於6月底重組資料中心團隊，以火箭和Starlink工程人員替換部分傳統資料中心管理人員，過去一個月已有逾300名SpaceX工程師加入相關專案。報道稱，部分現有設施目前也在重新設計。SpaceX預計不會完全放棄xAI的快速建設模式，但將更加重視冗餘和標準化。此前xAI曾在122天內讓首座配備10萬塊GPU的資料中心上線，但其初期備份系統不足，並長期依賴燃氣輪機等臨時設施。上週，孟菲斯一座大型資料中心又因施工影響電力線路發生停電，導致部分Grok模型及外部算力客戶受到影響，馬斯克隨後表示公司正在採取糾正措施以防止類似事件再次發生。

【美債收益率全線上揚 市場聚焦30年期標售和財政部回購】隨著油價延續升勢，促使交易員進一步押注美聯儲最快將在下週加息，美國國債收益率升至新的多年高位。各期限美債收益率上漲6至8個基點，其中30年期美債收益率升至2007年以來最高水平，2年期美債收益率則自2024年以來首次突破4.5%。交易員將美聯儲下週加息的機率提高至約70%，並已完全計入最遲10月加息的預期，此前為12月。美債遭拋售推高了紐約時間下午一點30年期美債標售的預期收益率。本次將續發行220億美元的30年期國債，隱含收益率約為5.35%，高於2001年以來歷次30年期美債標售的中標收益率。一小時後，美國財政部計劃回購最多60億美元10年至20年期美債，回購規模已從最初的20億美元上調。財政部8月19日出人意料地宣佈擴大回購規模，旨在抑制長期美債收益率上升。

Pound Slips as Dollar Gains and BoE Hike Bets Rise — The British pound weakened to around $1.35 as the dollar strengthened, despite growing expectations for further Bank of England rate hikes amid mounting inflation concerns. Rising energy prices, alongside the ECB’s rate increase and more hawkish inflation outlook, added to pressure on UK yields. Brent crude climbed above $100 a barrel, while UK natural gas prices reached their highest level since late 2022 as escalating Middle East tensions heightened concerns over energy supplies. Markets are now fully pricing four BoE rate increases by the end of 2027, despite Governor Andrew Bailey pushing back against the view that another hike is inevitable and stressing that future decisions will depend on evolving economic and geopolitical developments. Meanwhile, the dollar strengthened further after stronger-than-expected US PPI data lifted the implied probability of a Fed rate hike next week to above 70%.

UK Gilt Yield Hits 19-Year High as BoE Hike Bets Surge — The UK 10-year gilt yield jumped above 5.3%, its highest level since August 2007, as expectations for further Bank of England rate hikes intensified amid mounting inflation concerns. Rising energy prices, alongside the ECB’s rate increase and more hawkish inflation outlook, added to pressure on UK yields. Brent crude climbed above $100 a barrel, while UK natural gas prices reached their highest level since late 2022 as escalating Middle East tensions raised concerns over energy supplies. Markets are now fully pricing four BoE rate increases by the end of 2027, despite Governor Andrew Bailey pushing back against the view that another hike is inevitable, stressing that future decisions will depend on evolving economic and geopolitical developments. In the Eurozone, the ECB delivered its expected rate hike, raised its inflation forecasts for the next two years and warned that inflation could remain above its 2% target for an “extended period.”

【IAEA observes construction at Pikeh Zoushan; tunnel complex not inspected】The IAEA said it has observed construction activity at Pikeh Zoushan, a heavily fortified site south of Tehran's main uranium-enrichment plant suspected of nuclear-related activity. Silver Slides as Fed Rate Hike Bets Surge — Silver tumbled around 4% to $64.5 an ounce on Thursday as traders increased their bets on a Federal Reserve rate hike next week following stronger-than-expected US producer price data. US PPI rose 0.4% in August, while annual producer inflation accelerated to 5.4%, surpassing market expectations of 5.3%, driven by a sharp rise in energy prices, alongside evidence of stronger cost pass-through across other sectors. The data came as oil prices rallied again amid escalating US-Iran strikes in the Middle East, reinforcing expectations for tighter monetary policy and prompting markets to price in a more than 70% chance of a Federal Reserve rate hike on September 16. Meanwhile, traders also increased their bets on further European Central Bank tightening after the central bank raised borrowing costs as expected and warned that inflation risks remained tilted to the upside.

【何立峰出席礦業國際合作部長論壇暨2026中國國際礦業大會開幕式並在天津調研 】中共中央政治局委員、國務院副總理何立峰10日在天津出席礦業國際合作部長論壇暨2026中國國際礦業大會開幕式並致辭。何立峰指出，近年來，在習近平主席提出的構建人類命運共同體理念的科學指引下，中國政府深入推進全球礦業合作，以實際行動踐行四大全球倡議，探索出一條開放包容、合作共贏的高質量發展之路，與各國建立了相互尊重、和平共處、互利共贏的良好合作關係，共同取得了豐碩成果。何立峰指出，礦業國際合作是實現各國資源優勢互補、促進經濟社會發展的重要載體，也是維護全球產供鏈安全穩定的現實需要。中國始終充分尊重各國礦產資源主權，支援各國自主選擇符合本國國情的資源開發、產業發展道路，願同各國深化全方位務實合作，共享礦業發展機遇，共同開創互利共贏、綠色繁榮的全球礦業美好未來。開幕式前，何立峰到礦山機械裝備博覽會展館巡館。在津期間，何立峰還調研了中新天津生態城、天津濱海－中關村科技園、國家級經開區、綜合保稅區建設運營情況。他指出，要深入實施京津冀協同發展戰略，發揮政策和區位優勢，加快推進城市更新，積極推動各類開放載體轉型升級，支援發展跨境電商、融資租賃、物流倉儲等業態，在推動高水平開放、高質量發展上不斷取得新成效。（新華社）

Mining stocks came under heavy pressure, with Antofagasta and Anglo American falling more than 5%, while Endeavour, Glencore and Rio Tinto each declined by more than 3%. Meanwhile, the European Central Bank raised interest rates by 25 basis points, in line with market expectations. US 10-Year Yield Rises Past 4.9% — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note rose to 4.9% on Thursday as soaring energy prices supported the case for a Federal Reserve rate hike. New data showed that producer prices in the US rose 0.4% from the previous month in August with a surge in energy prices combining with signs of pass-through costs in broader sectors. The data coincided with a fresh rally in oil and product prices amid strikes between the US and Iran in the Middle East, prolonging the blockade of tankers in the Persian Gulf as major economies are forced to refill oil inventory. Yields were higher despite the Treasury's tripling of the notes and bonds buyback today. Besides the impact of higher energy prices, longer-term borrowing costs have been supported by record-setting corporate debt supply, with AI companies issuing north of $1.5 trillion in new issuance. On top of that, pressure on the Japanese yen drove Tokyo to repeatedly sell Treasury securities to defend the currency.

Brent Tops $105 — Brent crude rose above $105 a barrel on Thursday, reaching its highest level since May 19, as escalating tensions in the Middle East raised fears of prolonged disruption to global energy supplies. Iran signalled it will continue retaliatory strikes if US attacks persist, while Iran-backed Houthi forces are targeting Saudi assets and threatening shipping routes. Saudi Arabia’s crude oil production fell sharply in August 2026, dropping by around 1.9 million barrels a day to 6.238 million barrels a day, its lowest level since 1990. The deteriorating backdrop from the Middle East dims expectations that supply from the region could be normalized as Asian economies are forced to re-enter the energy market. China imported 8.93 bpd of crude oil in August, a 6.2% increase from July, after its reliance on inventory since the start of the conflict shielded the global economy from a deeper energy shortage due to blockaded tankers in the Persian Gulf.

USD Appreciates on Thursday — The dollar index rose to 99 on Thursday, as another rally in oil prices amid escalating hostilities between the US and Iran prompted investors to increase bets on a Fed rate hike next week. Markets are now pricing a 70% chance of a 25bps increase, up from 60% yesterday, while a hike is fully priced in for October. Meanwhile, the PPI report did little to alter expectations for a Fed hike, but confirmed that energy prices continued to fuel inflationary pressures. Headline producer inflation accelerated to 0.4% mom while core PPI rose 0.2% mom, below both the previous month’s 0.3% increase and forecasts of 0.3%. DAX Extends Losses — Frankfurt's DAX 40 extended early losses, falling 0.7% to around 25,400 on Thursday afternoon, a level last seen in late July. Traders digested the ECB’s latest decision, with policymakers raising rates by 25 basis points as expected and warning of continued upside risks to inflation stemming from the Middle East conflict. The move fueled expectations of further tightening. In the meantime, oil prices surged to fresh highs, as escalating US-Iran hostilities stoked fears of a prolonged conflict. Consumer cyclicals, software and technology stocks, and industrials faced the heaviest losses. Hochtief led losses among stocks, dropping 5.1%, with Adidas, Zalando and Siemens Energy also posting steep declines. SAP slipped 2.7%, as traders were cautious ahead of the quarterly earnings reports from the two IT and software giants, Oracle and Adobe. On the gainers side, shares of Deutsche Borse (1.9%), Hannover Ruck (1.8%) and Munchener Ruck (1.5%) stood out.

Swiss Bond Yield Climbs to Over Three-Month High — Switzerland's 10-year government bond yield rose above 0.5%, reaching its highest level since late May, as brent prices surged past the $100 mark amid a reduced oil supply outlook that further lifted concerns over inflation. Swiss inflation doubled to 0.8% in August, although remaining well within the SNB’s target and highlighting the limited pass through of higher global oil prices. Electricity prices are also set to decline around 4% next year, according to the Swiss Federal Electricity Commission, as local utility companies pass on lower wholesale costs to consumers. Meanwhile, quarterly economic growth was confirmed at a 5-year high of 1.5% On the monetary policy front, a Swiss Bankers Association survey showed that all bankers expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% by year-end. Markets anticipated the first rate hike in June 2027, while most economists expect the first hike in early 2028.

US Initial Unemployment Claims Hold Lower Trend — The number of people claiming unemployment benefits eased by 1,000 from the previous month to 206,000 on the first week of September 2026, loosely aligned with expectations of 205,000 to hold the trend of low claim counts since dropping to the near 60-year low of 189,000 in the middle of July. Bund Yield Hits 15-Year High as ECB Hikes Rates Again — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield climbed above 3.45%, reaching its highest level since April 2011, as investors digested the ECB’s latest rate decision while a renewed oil rally intensified inflation concerns and fueled expectations of further monetary tightening. The ECB delivered its second rate hike since the US-Iran war began, warning that inflation is likely to remain well above its 2% target for an extended period. It kept its 2026 inflation forecast at 3.0% but raised projections for 2027 and 2028 to 2.5% and 2.1%, respectively. Meanwhile, GDP growth forecasts were upgraded to 0.9% for 2026 and 1.4% for 2027, while the 2028 forecast remained unchanged at 1.5%. Renewed energy-price pressures are adding to the inflation outlook, with Brent crude hitting $105 a barrel and European gas prices reaching fresh three-and-a-half-year highs amid escalating Middle East tensions. Markets are now pricing in another ECB rate hike by December, with further tightening expected in 2027.

### 2026-09-10 20:33:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 3

US Producer Prices Accelerate — Producer prices in the US increased 0.4% mom in August 2026, following an upwardly revised 0.1% rise in July and in line with expectations. It is the biggest increase in three months, led by a 1.1% jump in prices of goods after declines in each of the previous two months, with diesel fuel jumping 24.1%. Prices for gasoline, jet fuel, home heating oil, candy and nuts, and tobacco products also advanced. In contrast, prices for residential electric power fell 0.5%. Meanwhile, services cost edged up 0.1%, led by a 2% rise in prices for truck transportation of freight. Increases were also seen for airline passenger services, legal services, hospital inpatient care, and automobiles retailing. Year-on-year, producer inflation accelerated to 5.4% from 4.8%, slightly above forecasts of 5.3%. Excluding food and energy, producer prices increased 0.2% on the month and 4.6% on the year, compared to 0.3% and 4.3% respectively in the previous month and forecasts of 0.3% and 4.6% respectively.

US Futures Pull Back Further — US equity futures were mostly lower as markets refrained from assuming more risk ahead of the US PPI release. Contracts for the S&P 500 inched down and those for the Nasdaq dropped 0.7%, while the Dow was flat. Producer prices are expected to have jumped 0.4% from the previous month in August as the war in Iran lifted wholesale energy prices. Oil and fuel benchmarks have gained further since the start of September on fresh escalation in the war and the increase in buying from Asia, adding to pressure on equities amid the possibility of a Federal Reserve rate hike next week. Chip producers fell amid their exposure to long-dated credit costs, with Marvell, Intel, and Lam Research down 3% premarket. Nvidia dropped 1% and Oracle fell 1% ahead of its earnings after the bell.

### 2026-09-10 19:54:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Turkey #Currency #Importance 1

Turkish Lira Holds Devaluation Pace — The Turkish lira weakened to a record low of 48.5 per USD in September, maintaining the controlled and slow devaluation rate by the Central Bank of Turkey as it intervenes in foreign exchange markets to prevent a slide. The TCMB held its key policy rate unchanged for the fifth straight decision in September, as expected. The central bank flagged that leading indicators pointed to a deceleration in underlying inflation in recent months, balanced by higher inflationary risks triggered by escalation in the Middle East war. The central bank had continued to sell liras in the open market to maintain the steady pace of devaluation. Support for the currency was also seen with policymakers downplaying an imminent return to one-week repo auctions, which has been suspended since March, forcing financial institutions to use the more expensive overnight rate.

### 2026-09-10 19:29:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Industrial Production #Importance 1

South Africa Factory Activity Stages Surprise Recovery — Manufacturing activity in South Africa rose by 1.1% year-on-year in July 2026, reversing the upwardly revised 1.7% drop in June and better than the expected 1.6% decline. The rebound followed three consecutive months of declining activity. The largest positive contributions came from the food and beverages division, which grew by 4.1%, and the petroleum, chemical products, rubber and plastic products division, which increased by 3.2%. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, manufacturing production grew by 2.2%, the most since May 2025, after a downwardly revised 0.8% increase in the month. South Africa manufacturing production increased by 1.0% in the three months ended July 2026 compared with the previous three months.

### 2026-09-10 19:11:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Turkey Holds Base Interest Rate at 37% — The Central Bank of the Republic of Turkey maintained its benchmark overnight lending rate at 37% for the fifth straight decision in its September 2026 meeting, as expected by markets. Then central bank noted that despite recent volatility in financial markets and the turbulent backdrop of the global macroeconomy, leading indicators suggested that the underlying inflation trend has moderated since the last meeting in June. On the other hand, the surging energy prices due to fresh escalation in the Iran-US war heightened inflationary risks, preventing any signals to warrant eased financial conditions or a favorable backdrop for the lira and overall emerging market currencies. The bank also nodded at defending the lira's stability, which was tested in May after Turkish courts removed the country's opposition leader Ozel, and previously prompted tweaks in the monetary framework to force commercial banks on higher funding rates.

### 2026-09-10 19:07:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Kosovo #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Kosovo Inflation Rate Accelerates in August — The annual inflation rate in Kosovo climbed to 7.3% in August 2026 from 6.5% in the prior month. Inflation accelerated sharply for transport, surging 21.1% after a 17.8% rise in July, while also accelerating for housing and utilities (11.8% vs 11.1%). Additional upward pressure was due to higier price growth in alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (5.1% vs 5.3%), restaurants and hotels (5.4% vs 5.3%), clothing and footwear (3% vs 2.8%), recreation, sports and culture (3.4% vs 2.3%), furnishings (3.4% vs 2.5%) and health (4.2% vs 4%). In contrast, food inflation slowed for the sixth consecutive month, easing to 2.6% from 2.7%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1.3%, the highest pace of increase in five months, up from 0.6% in July.

【防假冒防篡改 市場監管總局完善檢驗檢測報告編號查詢系統】記者今天瞭解到，市場監管總局（國家認監委）近日完成“檢驗檢測報告編號查詢系統”功能最佳化，恢復報告上傳及公眾查詢服務，並正式向社會開放。該系統具備區塊鏈存證、IP地址核驗、專用賬號管理等多項防假冒防篡改功能，並增加報告關鍵欄位資訊查驗內容，向社會公眾提供便捷、可信的檢驗檢測報告查詢服務。該系統引入區塊鏈存證與雜湊值校驗技術，檢驗檢測機構透過專用PC客戶端上傳報告電子原件時，系統將為每份報告自動生成唯一雜湊值並與報告編號繫結，確保報告內容不可篡改、可追溯。報告上傳實行“一機構一賬號”原則，機構使用唯一賬號進行登入上傳，新增郵箱校驗機制。同時，上傳資訊進一步豐富，在原有編號、機構名稱、簽發日期基礎上，增加委託人名稱、授權簽字人、機構聯絡方式等關鍵欄位，便利社會公眾精準查驗，市場監管部門也可透過管理端隨時調取原始檔案進行比對核驗。（央視新聞）

【電子資訊司召開電子元器件產業發展座談會】2026年9月10日，工業和資訊化部電子資訊司在京召開電子元器件產業發展座談會，部署“十五五”時期電子元器件產業發展有關工作。會上，工業和資訊化部電子資訊司圍繞“十五五”時期電子元器件產業發展需要，研究部署推動電子元器件行業管理相關工作。各參會單位結合自身工作領域，進行了交流發言，並就產業發展面臨的形勢和問題提出了意見建議。會議指出，新一輪科技革命和產業變革加速演進，電子元器件作為支撐資訊科技發展的基石作用日益凸顯。做好電子元器件產業基礎梳理和產業發展技術脈絡分析，對於研判“十五五”時期產業發展路徑、明確重點方向、最佳化政策供給具有重要意義。會議強調，要準確把握“十五五”時期電子元器件產業發展面臨的新形勢新任務，堅持系統觀念，加強統籌協調，紮實做好各項基礎性研究工作。要凝聚行業合力，充分發揮行業協會、研究機構和重點企業的各自優勢，形成上下聯動、協同推進的工作格局，持續推動電子元器件產業高質量發展。有關部屬事業單位、行業協會和平臺機構相關同志參加會議。(工信部)

### 2026-09-10 18:55:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Belarus #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Belarus Inflation Rate Edges Up — The annual inflation rate in Belarus edged up to 4.5% in August 2026 from 4.4% in the prior month. Services inflation rose to 5.7% from 5.4%, reflecting higher price growth for housing and utilities, which edged up to 10.4% from 10.3%, and transports, where growth accelerated to 6.1% from 5.3%. Additional upward pressure was due to higher inflation for furnishings (3.6% vs 2.8%), information and communication (2.5% vs 1.8%) and restaurants and accomodation (7.8% vs 6.8%). Meanwhile, inflation remained unchanged at 3.1% for overall food products, although cost growth for food and non-alcoholic beverages eased slightly to 2.2% from 2.3%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices declined 0.08%, following a 0.3% rise in July.

### 2026-09-10 18:38:51  #市場 #央行 #國際

【歐美英三大央行議息前瞻 美聯儲加息陷入兩難】本週開始，歐洲央行、美聯儲和英國央行將陸續召開議息會議。在油價重回100美元的關口時，三家央行會如何行動？市場對於歐洲央行、美聯儲和英國央行的行動預期卻出現較大的分化。 

路透社調查顯示，經濟學家們普遍預計，歐洲央行會加息25個基點。首先，歐洲這一輪通脹上升，很大程度來自能源相關的供給端因素。其次，如果歐洲央行加息，歐洲和美國之間的利差發生變化，可能影響歐元和美元的走勢。第三。如果歐洲央行進一步收緊貨幣政策，全球債券收益率可能繼續面臨上行壓力，美國的長期融資成本也可能受到影響。 

單純從通脹壓力來看，美聯儲進一步加息的理由也正在增加。但是芝加哥商品交易所美聯儲利率觀測工具資料顯示，目前市場預計美聯儲在下週議息會議上加息25個基點的機率大約只有六成，而且最近一段時間波動明顯。

美國近期公佈的就業資料好於預期，但在AI投資熱潮等多重因素推動下，美國長期融資成本仍然處於高位，美聯儲是否需要透過加息進一步收緊金融環境，面臨一定不確定性。另一方面，9月份議息會議的時間點也比較敏感，再過不久就是美國重要的中期選舉。在副總統萬斯公開喊話後，總統特朗普也施壓，以貿易政策威脅，不僅要求美聯儲不要加息，甚至希望轉而降息。（央視財經）

### 2026-09-10 18:34:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #Portugal #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Portugal Inflation Accelerates to 3.3% in August — Portugal’s annual inflation rate accelerated to 3.3% in August 2026, up from 3.0% in July, reaching its highest level since April. The increase was driven primarily by a sharp acceleration in transport prices, which rose 6.4% year-on-year, compared with 4.9% in July, largely reflecting higher fuel costs amid escalating tensions in the Middle East. Additional pressure came from miscellaneous goods and services (4.5% vs 4.1%), financial and insurance services (3.1% vs 2.6%), restaurants and hotels (6.2% vs 6.0%), and housing and utilities (3.3% vs 3.2%). Meanwhile, inflation eased for food and non-alcoholic beverages (2.1% vs 2.2%) and recreation and culture (1.5% vs 2.4%). Core inflation, which excludes volatile components such as energy and unprocessed food, remained unchanged at 2.6%. The EU-harmonized inflation rate accelerated to 3.6%, its highest level since May 2024 and well above the European Central Bank’s 2% inflation target.

Seven OPEC+ members, including Russia and other allies, had agreed to raise output in August but were unable to do so due to the conflict. The survey is based on LSEG flow data, Kpler and other flow trackers, and reporting from oil companies, OPEC and consultants.

Crude Oil Extends Rally — Crude oil rose past $97 per barrel on Thursday, the highest in over three months and soaring nearly 15% since the start of September, as escalatory strikes between Iran and the US further supressed supply from the key region. Tehran signaled it will not back down from a military blockade as the US launched attacks against a series of Iranian tankers near Kharg Island, host to the state's key energy infrastructure. The deteriorating backdrop from the Middle East dims expectations that supply from the region could be normalized as Asian economies are forced to re-enter the energy market. China imported 8.93 bpd of crude oil in August, a 6.2% increase from July, after its reliance on inventory since the start of the conflict had shielded the global economy from a deeper energy shortage due to blockaded tankers in the Persian Gulf. Inventories were also lower in the US, with the government's SPR hovering at a record low.

### 2026-09-10 18:16:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Ireland Inflation Rate Hits 4-Month High — Ireland’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.7% in August 2026, the highest in four months, from 3.4% in each of the previous two months. Prices increased at a faster pace for housing and utilities (8.5% vs 7.7% in July), furnishings and household equipment (0.3% vs 0.1%), transportation (5.4% vs 2.1%), recreation and culture (1.7% vs 0.5%), and miscellaneous goods and services (2.9% vs 2.6%). Moreover, price growth remained steady for health (at 1.8%) and education (at 8.9%). Meanwhile, price growth slowed for food and non-alcoholic beverages (0.1% vs 0.7%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.3% vs 2.5%), clothing and footwear (3.1% vs 8.5%), information and communication (1.3% vs 1.4%), restaurants and hotels (3.4% vs 3.6%), and insurance and financial services (4.7% vs 5.5%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices grew by 0.7% in August, following a 0.1% increase in the preceding period.

### 2026-09-10 18:15:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Serbia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Central Bank Serbia Holds Rates Steady — The National Bank of Serbia held its key policy rate unchanged at 5.75% at its September 2026 meeting, leaving borrowing costs at the same level since September of 2024, citing caution over the impact of the international developments on actual and expected inflation trends. Headline inflation remained below the midpoint of the target range of 3±1.5%, having eased to 1.9% in July, reflecting greater resilience than expected by policymakers. Inflation is expected to fluctuate around the 4% level from September, where it will remain throughout 2027. Economic activity also improved in the second quarter of 2026, accelerating past estimates to 3.8%, while economic growth is projected at 3.2% in 2026 and 4.5% in 2027. On the labor market, unemployment is at 7.2%, its lowest level on record. However, policymakers highlighted elevated uncertainty regarding the conflict in the Middle East, while spillover effects cannot be rulled out.

【Bond market on edge ahead of ECB decision; German 10-year hits highest since 2011】Euro-area government bond yields held near multi-year highs on Thursday as investors awaited the European Central Bank policy decision. Analysts expect a 25bp hike, no fresh guidance and a reiteration of data dependence. Germany’s 10-year yield rose 0.5bp to 3.44% after touching 3.4514%, its highest since April 2011. Some market participants say rate-hike expectations may be overdone because energy-driven growth drag could help curb inflation; they warn that a dovish ECB tone could send borrowing costs sharply lower. Traders price the ECB deposit rate at 2.74% by December, up from 2.25% today—implying two more 25bp hikes—and expect 3.10% by Sept 2027, fully pricing a third hike and implying roughly a 40% chance of a fourth. A strategist at a German commercial bank, Erik Liem, said Lagarde’s response to recent bond moves will be watched closely and that even a small dovish hint could have a large market impact today.

### 2026-09-10 18:10:55  #MKT News

【Trump again promises cash payouts; years of tariff revenue unlikely to fill trillion-dollar gap】President Trump on Wednesday reiterated a pledge to pay $5,000 to each U.S. adult if Republicans win both chambers; Vice President Vance said funds could come from tariffs. Covering roughly 240m adults would cost about $1.2tr, roughly two-thirds of the FY25 $1.8tr deficit. The Yale Budget Lab estimates current tariff schedules would raise about $1.9tr over 10 years — annual receipts would be far too small to fund a one‑off payout, implying incremental borrowing and higher publicly held debt. At about 8% of median full‑time annual earnings, a $5,000 payment could materially boost consumption if more than 60% is spent, adding upward pressure to inflation; headline inflation is near 3.4%. Markets price roughly a 60% chance of a Fed rate hike this month, which could partially offset fiscal stimulus. The proposal requires congressional approval; prediction markets assign only about a 15% probability Republicans win both chambers, leaving large execution risk.

### 2026-09-10 18:05:30  #其他

【章魚動力聯合創始人樊慶元：閉環是早期捷徑，具身智慧終將走向產業分工】當前具身智慧賽道熱度空前，資本、技術、人才快速湧入，行業普遍以全棧自研、垂直閉環作為現階段的發展選擇。但在產業熱潮背後，技術路線尚未收斂、行業標準尚未成型、落地場景仍在探索，產業究竟會長期保持垂直自研狀態，還是終將走向專業化分工，成為行業核心爭議點。 在由聚合智慧產業創新中心、湖北科投聯合主辦的聚合智慧產業發展大會2026上，章魚動力聯合創始人樊慶元在接受《中國經營報》等媒體專訪時給出了清晰判斷：腦手資料一體化閉環，是具身產業早期快速迭代的最優捷徑，但絕非行業終局。隨著技術成熟、標準清晰，產業必然走向分工協同。 成立僅八個月的章魚動力，是賽道中快速崛起的新銳力量。自今年1月創立以來，企業在極短週期內搭建起覆蓋大模型、物理AI、硬體研發、複雜系統交付的完整團隊，並快速落地世界基礎模型、仿生靈巧手、多模態數採三大核心產品，形成獨特的“腦—手—資料”自研閉環。在樊慶元看來，短期閉環是創業企業搶抓產業視窗、快速驗證技術路徑、補齊物理AI生產力短板的務實選擇，而長期分工協同，才是聚合智慧產業規模化、降本化、產業化的必然趨勢。（中國經營報）

### 2026-09-10 17:57:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Oman #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Oman Inflation Accelerates to Three-Month High — Oman’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.4% in August 2026, up from 3.2% in July and reaching its highest level since May. The acceleration was driven primarily by a sharp increase in transport prices, which climbed 8.5% year-on-year, compared with 6.5% in July, as fuel costs rose amid higher crude prices and escalating US-Iran tensions. Additional upward pressure came from miscellaneous goods and services, where inflation accelerated to 6.1% from 5.3%. Meanwhile, food inflation eased slightly to 7.0% from 7.3% but remained elevated. Price growth was unchanged for restaurants and hotels at 3.6%, as well as for furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance at 3.1%. By contrast, housing and utilities prices fell for a second consecutive month, declining 0.6%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices decreased 0.3% in August, following a 0.7% increase in July.

### 2026-09-10 17:48:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Mining Production #Importance 1

South Africa Mining Output Falls More Than Expected — Mining production in South Africa plunged by 7.5% year-on-year in July 2026, after an upwardly revised 4.3% drop in June and worse than analysts' forecasts of a 2.8% slump. The latest decline extended South Africa’s mining downturn to three consecutive months and was the sharpest since February 2025. The industry continues to face structural constraints, including logistics bottlenecks, high operating costs, ageing mines and weaker demand for some commodities like iron ore and coal. Output declined across almost all categories, with platinum group metals (PGMs) (-13.5%), coal (-7.5%) and iron ore (-8%) making the largest negative contributions. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, mining production shrank by 1.9%, after a downwardly revsied 0.1% increase in the month before. Mining production slipped by 5.5% in the three months ended July 2026 compared with the previous three months.

### 2026-09-10 17:47:20  #MKT News #EURUSD #Impact mixed #DXY #US10Y

【ECB preview: 25bp hike to 2.50% fully priced; Lagarde's press conference to set tone】ECB is expected to raise the deposit rate 25bps to 2.50% — the move is already fully priced; markets will treat President Lagarde's press conference as the main source of new information. Eurozone headline inflation accelerated to 3.3% YoY in August, with energy and geopolitics pushing gas to the highest levels since 2022; core and services inflation are continuing to slow, while economic activity has shown surprising resilience. The hike will put the deposit rate at the top of the ECB's estimated neutral range. Statement language is unlikely to change materially; meeting-by-meeting, data-dependent wording and the current risk-balance framing are expected to remain. Lagarde's tone is the critical market pivot: a hawkish tone would underline a persistent energy shock, upside inflation risks and scope for further tightening; a dovish tone would stress easing core/services inflation and risks of over-tightening via tighter financial conditions. Prognosis for forecasts: markets expect upward revisions to growth; inflation outlooks diverge — Goldman Sachs sees modest 2026 downside but brings 2027 headline inflation up to 2.7% (core 2.6%); UniCredit leans hawkish; MUFG flags 2026 revisions and warns forecasts may be outdated. Key policy question is whether September marks the end of the hiking cycle — MUFG and ING lean terminal in September, while Goldman Sachs, Wells Fargo and UniCredit see material upside risk if energy prices remain high; most banks do not expect an explicit commitment to further hikes. Market pricing implies ~48bps more tightening by year-end and ~85bps by end-2027; a dovish Lagarde could prompt downshifts in tightening expectations and pressure the euro, while a hawkish signal would be needed to support EUR. Near-term risk: US CPI and the FOMC could quickly re-center market attention and dominate EUR/USD and global rates moves.

### 2026-09-10 17:41:20  #MKT News

【NBS issues revised Measures for Classifying Statistical Units; statistics to follow location of economic activity from 2027】On Sept. 10 the National Bureau of Statistics published revised Measures for Classifying Statistical Units and Specific Handling, effective Jan. 1, 2027. The revision replaces the prior “on-site” statistical principle with a “location of economic activity” principle and adds definitions for “location of economic activity” and rules for identifying a unit’s “primary location of main economic activity.” Where a unit has multiple locations, the primary location is normally the site with the largest share of operating revenue (wholesale and retail use gross margin). For new-economy cases, units conducting economic activity on internet platforms will be counted under the location-of-economic-activity principle; online and offline activities follow the same statistical standard.

### 2026-09-10 17:41:15  #其他

【國家統計局公佈新版《統計單位劃分及具體處理辦法》 2027年起按照經營活動發生地原則統計】9月10日，國家統計局公佈修訂後的《統計單位劃分及具體處理辦法》，2027年1月1日起實施。《辦法》修改了統計單位的統計原則，將原有的“按照在地原則進行統計”明確為“按照經營活動發生地原則進行統計”。新增對“經營活動發生地”具體內涵和“主要經營活動的發生地”判定辦法的規定。具體來看，經營活動發生地是指統計單位開展經營活動的場所。存在多處經營活動發生地的，原則上將營業收入（批發和零售業採用毛利）份額最高的場所確定為主要經營活動的發生地。對於新經濟業態，《辦法》規定，在網際網路平臺開展經濟活動的單位，按照“經營活動發生地原則”進行統計，線上活動與線下活動遵循相同的統計原則。

【Pakistan says no immediate military response to Houthi attacks; will act under Mecca Agreement when 'time comes'】Pakistan said on Thursday it is not discussing a military response to Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia but will act under the Mecca Agreement "when the time comes", foreign ministry spokesman Sajjad Haider Khan said at a weekly briefing. The joint defence pact signed last month with Saudi Arabia and Turkey says an attack on any of the three states will be treated as an attack on all. Khan’s comments followed Houthi strikes this week on Saudi cities and energy infrastructure that Reuters said have opened a second front in the Middle East. Islamabad has warned Iran to restrain its Houthi ally and condemned the attacks but gave no detail on how it might fulfil obligations under the pact.

### 2026-09-10 17:31:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Croatia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Croatia Producer Prices at 3-Month High — Producer prices in Croatia increased by 6.2% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from 4.9% in the previous month and marking the highest reading since May. Costs in mining and quarrying advanced sharply (28.7% vs 13.9% in July), reflecting higher prices in the extraction of crude petroleum and natural gas (54.1% vs 19.5%). Additionally, manufacturing prices continued to rise (5.6% vs 4.3%), particularly for the manufacture of coke and refined petroleum (59.6% vs 38.7%), basic metals (6.2% vs 3.6%), chemicals and chemical products (6.0% vs 5.2%), wearing apparel (2.1% vs 1.5%), and wood and wood products, except furniture, articles of straw and plaiting materials (6.5% vs 6.1%). Prices for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply also edged higher (5.6% vs 5.4%). On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 1.3% in August, recovering from a 0.2% fall in the previous month.

### 2026-09-10 17:30:41  #Trading Economics #Economy #Greece #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Greece Industrial Output Falls in July — Industrial output in Greece declined by 1.2% from the previous year in July 2026, reversing from an upwardly revised 1.2% increase in June. The downturn was driven by sharp contractions in electricity supply (-12.4% vs -3.9%), and water supply (-4.0% vs -2.6%) declined. Mining and quarrying also remained in contraction, declining 3.5% (vs. -4.3%). Manufacturing, however, remained in positive territory, although growth eased (1.9% vs 2.6%). Notable growth was recorded in computers, electronic and optical products (13.6%), machinery and equipment (7.3%), printing and reproduction of recorded products (6.4%), and motor vehicles, trailers and semi-trailers (5.1%), while wood and wood products (-7.0%) and leather and related products (-6.2%) saw the sharpest declines. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial activity declined further to -1.7% from -0.3% in the previous month.

【Israeli media: Houthi capture of Mocha could reshape regional balance】Israeli outlet the Jerusalem Post reported on Thursday that Houthi forces seized the Red Sea port city of Mocha. Mocha lies at the northern approach to the Bab al-Mandeb Strait; Houthi control moves Iran-backed fighters closer to a key chokepoint, raising the risk to Red Sea shipping and energy transit. The capture, while not unexpected, may broaden Tehran’s leverage in the region despite Iranian setbacks elsewhere and increase geopolitical uncertainty that could disrupt energy markets and shipping liquidity. Former US president Trump said on the 9th he expected US‑Iran tensions to ease after the US midterms and for oil to fall; the Mocha offensive could challenge that view. The Yemeni government’s loss highlights that the long-running conflict still has potential to alter regional dynamics.

### 2026-09-10 17:25:02  #MKT News

【Goldman Sachs: Shareholder returns set to become a new driver for South Korean equities】Goldman Sachs said in a Sept. 9 report that shareholder returns are likely to increasingly drive South Korean equity performance, complementing earnings growth and helping reverse perceptions of weak corporate capital returns. Policy moves — a “corporate value enhancement” plan, dividend tax incentives and proposed tax changes for buybacks — are shifting from voluntary disclosure toward substantive incentives and stronger accountability for capital allocation and payouts. On fundamentals, consensus earnings and free cash flow forecasts have been revised up while corporate balance sheets retain ample cash, supporting larger dividends and buybacks without materially pressuring capex or balance-sheet quality. Buybacks and treasury-stock cancellations are accelerating and high-dividend opportunities are broadening. Goldman expects aggregate dividends plus buybacks to rise sharply in 2026–27, lifting total shareholder return to roughly 9% in 2026 and about 14% in 2027.

### 2026-09-10 17:21:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Current Account #Importance 1

South Africa Logs Widest Current Account Gap Since 2015 — South Africa posted a current account deficit of ZAR 205.5 billion in Q2 2026, the widest since Q4 2015, compared to a surplus of ZAR 181.6 billion in the previous period. The quarter was the first to fully capture the impact of the war in Iran, which pushed up energy prices and import costs. The trade surplus narrowed sharply to ZAR 146.4 billion from ZAR 428.8 billion in Q1, as the value of merchandise imports far outpaced that of merchandise and net gold exports. The increase in the import bill was driven largely by an 82% surge in the value of crude oil imports, reflecting South Africa’s high reliance on imported fuel. Concurrently, the shortfall on the services, income and current transfer account widened sharply to ZAR 351.9 billion from ZAR 247.2 billion, mainly attributed to a substantial increase in the primary income deficit. As a ratio of GDP, the country's current account balance shifted to a deficit of 2.6% in Q2 from a surplus of 2.3% in Q1.
